Professor McGonagall called out the names one by one.\n\n\"Potter, Harry!\"\n\nA small, bespectacled boy with a lightning-shaped scar stepped forward. Harry closed his eyes, his fingers tapping rapidly against his knees.\n\n**Sorting Hat:** *Hmm, difficult. Very difficult. Plenty of courage, I see. Not a bad mind, either. There's talent, oh yes. I like the way numbers fit together, and how words make patterns. I like knowing why things happen.*\n\n**Sorting Hat:** *Ah, a mind that craves understanding. A mind that sees the world differently. Ravenclaw!*\n\nThe Great Hall fell silent. Then, a murmur began, growing louder. The Boy-Who-Lived, in Ravenclaw? Unheard of!\n\nHarry stood, walked to the Ravenclaw table, and sat down. He didn't understand why people said one thing but meant another. He preferred the company of books to people, finding them less confusing.\n\nOne day, Professor Flitwick kept him after class. \"Harry, I've noticed your exceptional talent in Charms. But I've also noticed you seem... overwhelmed sometimes.\"\n\nHarry looked at the floor. \"It's just... there's so much. So many people, so much noise.
